The <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span> is that each face has probability <span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">1</sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">6</sub></span> on every roll.</p><p>How many degrees of freedom does this chi-square test have?</p>	three (3)	Incorrect	five (5)	Correct	twelve (12)	Incorrect	one hundred and thirty-one (131)	Incorrect
